  • Erasmus+ Internship Mobility program → [requires] → internship not ...
================

🤖 RAG ANSWER (66.0s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   To apply for the Erasmus internship program, students must first meet the
   GPA requirement (2.20 for undergraduates, 2.5 for graduates) and secure a
   host organization’s acceptance letter for a minimum 2-month internship.
   Application requirements and necessary documents (e.g., CV, transcript,
   acceptance letter) are published on the internal web and internship
   websites starting in late October. Undergraduate applicants must also have
   their language scores calculated based on ENG 101-102 or SPS and AL course
   grades. Students submit applications through the Online Application
   System, ensuring the Erasmus+ Internship Mobility Agreement is signed by
   the host organization. They must complete the Learning Agreement, Grant
   Agreement, and Travel and Insurance documents, upload them to the system,
   and notify the Internship Office. Selection is based on the Erasmus+
   Score, with the approved student list and grant allocations announced by
   mid-April. Final grant eligibility is confirmed by the end of April, and
   students must finalize agreements before starting their internship.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (71.6s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Erasmus öğrenim hareketliliğinde hibe, hareketlilik dönemi için geçen
   hibeli ay sayısı ile ilgili ev sahibi ülke için belirlenen aylık hibe
   miktarının çarpılmasıyla hesaplanır. Tamamlanmayan aylar varsa, bu aylar
   içinde geçen gün sayısı ile aylık birim masrafın 1/30’u çarpılarak
   hesaplanır. Hibenin toplam tutarı, hareketliliğin süresi ve Ulusal Ajans
   tarafından belirlenen aylık hibe miktarına göre değişir. Ayrıca, özel
   ihtiyaçlarla ilgili harcamalar için destekleyici belgeler ibraz edilerek
   geri ödeme yapılabilir. AB fonları ile ödenmiş olan giderler, Erasmus+
   hibesi ile tekrar karşılanamaz.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (66.3s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The Learning Agreement (LA) process for exchange students at Sabancı
   University involves several steps. The student prepares, signs, and
   submits the LA within the deadline set by the International Relations
   Office (IRO), ensuring it includes approved course selections, SU-
   equivalent credits, course types, and ECTS values determined by the
   exchange academic advisor. The advisor verifies that courses taken abroad
   replace those required at SU and approves any changes before the partner
   institution’s term ends. After obtaining the partner university’s
   signature, the student submits the signed LA to the IRO, which forwards it
   to the Faculty Administrative Officer for final approval by the Faculty
   Executive Committee or Faculty Administrative Board. Graduate and
   undergraduate students follow the same procedure, with the LA ensuring
   course recognition and compliance with SU graduation requirements.
   Students must also submit the signed LA, transcript, and attendance
   certificate upon return to complete the process.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (170.0s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The disciplinary objection process at Sabancı University allows students
   to object to sanctions within **15 days** of notification by submitting a
   written petition to the University Administrative Board. The board
   examines the objection and either accepts or rejects the sanction within
   **15 days**. If rejected, the disciplinary board or supervisor reviews the
   objection considering the rejection grounds. For dormitory-related
   punishments (e.g., removal), objections must be submitted within **5
   working days**, with the Disciplinary Board deciding within **7 working
   days**. If a student appeals to administrative jurisdiction, the stay of
   execution decision is notified to relevant parties **within 1 day** of the
   court order. Suspension periods for expelled students are excluded from
   the maximum study period, and suspended students must pay tuition for
   affected semesters.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (168.4s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Burs başvurusu, öğrencilerin ilan edilen tarihlerde Öğrenci Burs Başvurusu
   (ÖBS) üzerinden online yapmasıyla gerçekleşir. Üstün akademik başarı
   nedeniyle burs alabilecek öğrenciler için başvuruda bulunulmasına gerek
   yoktur; bu burslar değerlendirme sonucu otomatik olarak atanır. Lisansüstü
   programlara başvuran adaylar, kabul sürecinde burs talebini başvuru
   sistemi üzerinden iletir. Burs başvurusunda, aile gelir ve çalışma durumu
   belgeleri gibi maddi gereksinimi kanıtlamaya yönelik belgeler sunulması
   zorunludur. Lisansüstü öğrenciler, burs devamlılığı için her dönem başında
   Enstitü Yönetim Kurulu tarafından değerlendirilir. Ayrıca, burs
   güncellemeleri veya yeni talepler için lisansüstü öğrenciler, Enstitüye
   dönem başında başvuruda bulunur. Güz dönemi için en geç Ağustos ayının ilk
   haftası, Bahar dönemi için Ocak ayının ilk haftası içinde bu işlemler
   tamamlanmalıdır. Bazı durumlarda, başvurular Hababam sistemi üzerinden
   Aplications / Uygulamalar sekmesi ve Burs ve Mali Destek Programı
   üzerinden incelenir.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (281.6s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The Sabancı University Main Endowment Scholarship Fund is a financial aid
   initiative designed to support undergraduate and graduate students who
   demonstrate academic success and financial need. It accepts donations from
   Sabancı University employees, alumni, and other individuals or
   institutions through a formal contribution process. Employee donations are
   deducted from their monthly gross salary at a self-determined percentage
   or fixed amount, facilitated by completing the SUFORM or emailing
   bordro@sabanciuniv.edu. Additionally, international donors, particularly
   U.S. citizens, can contribute via the "Friends of Sabancı University Fund"
   under the Turkish Philanthropic Fund in New York to benefit from tax
   incentives. The fund’s resources include 90% of interest income from its
   assets and may utilize revenue from venue rentals, with administrative
   decisions made by the IPAR Director or Secretary General.
------------------------------------------------------------

   Disiplin cezası alan öğrencinin bursu, ceza türüne göre farklılık
   gösterir:  - **Kınama / 1 haftadan 1 aya kadar uzaklaştırma**: Burs

   dönemler için burs kesilir; bu dönemlerde öğrenim ücretinin %25’i ödenir.

   çıkarma**: Burs tamamen kesilir ve yeniden tahsis edilmez.  Lisansüstü
   öğrenciler için disiplin cezası alanların burs durumu, Enstitü Yönetim
   Kurulu tarafından tekrar değerlendirilir. Ayrıca, disiplin cezası
   nedeniyle burs kesintisi uygulanmış bir öğrenci mezun olursa, ceza süresi

🤖 RAG ANSWER (131.7s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Lisansüstü programlarda tekrarlanan derslerde GNO hesaplamasında,
   öğrencinin daha önce aldığı dersin puanı genel not ortalamasından
   çıkarılır ve en son alınan dersin puanı yeni GNO'ya dahil edilir. Tekrar
   edilen derslerden alınan en son not, GNO hesaplamalarında kullanılır. Notu
   B veya B'den yüksek olan dersler tekrar alınamaz. Öğrencinin kayıt yaptığı
   tüm dersler transkripte yansıtılır, ancak ortalama hesaplamasında sadece
   en son not dikkate alınır. GNO, öğrencinin üniversitedeki tüm derslerinin
   SÜ kredisiyle hesaplanan toplam puanına göre belirlenir.

================

🤖 RAG ANSWER (80.8s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   To open a graduate program, the Graduate School Board must submit a
   proposal with required details (courses, credits, program purpose) to the
   Academic Council (AC) for approval, following the University Education
   Council (UEC)’s opinion under the Vice President for Education (VP-E)
   coordination. The program begins after approval by the Council of Higher
   Education (YOK). Closing a graduate program involves the Graduate School
   Board proposing the closure, obtaining UEC and AC approval, and securing
   final approval from the Board of Trustees. Once approved, the SR unit
   removes the closed program from the YOKSIS system within one week of YOK’s
   confirmation. Changes to existing graduate programs (e.g., course
   requirements) follow a separate procedure outlined in the relevant course
   management guidelines.
------------------------------------------------------------

   How is GPA calculated at Sabancı University?

📚 SOURCE   : Lisans Yönetmeliği / lisans_yon_eng

✅ EXPECTED :
   GPA is calculated by multiplying the SU credit of each course by its grade
   coefficient, summing these total points, and dividing by the total SU
   credits taken. Averages are shown with two decimals. In academic ranking
   calculations, all digits after comma are taken into consideration. Courses
   taken at other universities during exchange and summer programs are
   included. For repeated courses, the last grade is used in GPA calculation.

[Gpa Calculation]

🔗 RELATIONS (From Knowledge Graph):

  • Sabancı University Graduate Education and Training Instruction Letter → [req...
================

🤖 RAG ANSWER (70.1s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   At Sabancı University, GPA (Grade Point Average) is calculated by dividing
   the total SU credit points (obtained by multiplying each course’s SU
   credit by its grade coefficient) by the total SU credits of all courses
   taken. For **undergraduate programs**, the GPA is displayed with two
   decimal places, and the Cumulative GPA (CGPA) includes all courses taken
   since enrollment, using the last grade for repeated courses. For
   **graduate programs**, the GPA for a term is calculated similarly, but the
   CGPA also aggregates all courses from enrollment, with academic rankings
   considering all decimal digits beyond two. Both undergraduate and graduate
   programs use the final grade of a repeated course in CGPA calculations.
------------------------------------------------------------

   inceleyip onaylar ve tekrar ÖK'ya gönderir.   Lisansüstü öğrenciler için
   tezsiz yüksek lisans programında GNO 3,00 ve proje dersinde başarılı olma
   şartı aranır. Güz dönemi mezuniyet başvuruları genellikle Kasım/Aralık

   "Öğrenci Transkript İstek Formu" doldurur. Basılı kopya talep edilirse,
   öğrencinin o yıl için belirlenen transkript ücretini Akbank Merkez Şube
   9999 hesabına yatırarak banka dekontunu ilgili forma yüklemesi gerekir.
   E-imzalı transkript ücretsiz olarak talep edilebilir. Başvuru yapıldıktan
   sonra ÖK Müdürü, transkriptin her sayfasını imzalar. Elektronik imzalı
   transkript, belge hazır olduktan sonra öğrencinin e-posta adresine
   gönderilir. Basılı transkript, zarfın ağzı kapalı olarak ÖK'dan teslim
   alınır. Öğrenci şahsen teslim alamazsa, belge normal posta veya karşı
   ödemeli kargo ile adrese gönderilir. Transkript, öğrencinin kayıtlı olduğu
   düzeydeki tüm dersleri, notlarını, DNO ve GNO'sunu içeren İngilizce resmi
   bir belgedir.
------------------------------------------------------------

================

🤖 RAG ANSWER (51.1s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Students must apply for a leave of absence by the last business day of the
   4th or 7th week following the first day of classes in the academic year
   after their registration. For a semester leave, applying by the 4th week
   requires paying 25% of the semester fee, while applying by the 7th week
   requires 50%. For an academic year leave, the payment is 25% or 50% of the
   annual tuition fee, depending on the application deadline. Applications
   must include documented proof for reasons such as health, financial,
   family, personal, academic issues, or unexpected situations. Students
   conscripted, arrested, condemned, or taken into custody must apply within
   2 months of the situation arising or finalization to be granted a leave
   for the period specified in relevant articles. Undergraduate students
   require approval from the related Faculty Administrative Board (Article
   39), while graduate students need approval from the Graduate School
   Administrative Board (Article 40). If a student returns during a leave,
   the paid leave fee is deducted from the tuition for the semester they re-
   enroll in.
------------------------------------------------------------

   The disciplinary penalties for students at Sabancı University include:  1.
   **Warnings, reprimands, and short-term suspensions** (1 week to 1 month)
   from the university, with disciplinary proceedings required to start
   **within 1 month** of the offense being reported.  2. **Suspensions for 1
   or 2 semesters** or **dismissal** from the university, with proceedings
   required to start **within 3 months** of the offense being reported.  3.
   **Campus access restrictions**: Students under suspension or dismissal are
   **prohibited from entering the university campus**.  4. **Financial
   consequences**: Students receiving suspension must **pay tuition for the
   suspended semester**.  5. **Scholarship evaluation**: Scholarship
   continuation for disciplined students is assessed under the Scholarship
   and Financial Support Instruction.  6. **Escalation for repeated
   offenses**: A repeated disciplinary violation results in a **heavier
   penalty**, potentially up to dismissal.  7. **Dormitory-specific
   penalties**: Warnings, reprimands, or removal from dormitories, with
   appeal processes defined for objections.   Suspension periods are **not
   counted toward the maximum study duration**, and disciplinary decisions
   are notified to relevant units, including scholarship providers and
   dormitory management.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (88.8s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Lisans öğrencileri, daha önce geçer not aldıkları dersleri **en çok üç
   dönem** (izinli dönemler ve yaz dönemleri hariç) içinde tekrarlayabilir.
   Daha önce "F", "U", "NA" veya "W" notu alan dersler tekrar edilerek
   başarılması gerekir ve bu derslerin tekrar sayısı, mezuniyet gereklerine
   göre belirlenir. Lisansüstü öğrencileri, "C", "C+" veya "B-" notu
   aldıkları dersleri program süresi boyunca tekrar edebilir, ancak "B",
   "B+", "S" veya "A" notu alan dersler tekrar edilemez. Tekrar edilen
   dersler doğrudan transkripte kaydedilir ve GNO hesaplamalarında son not
   geçerlidir. Öğrencilere bir dönemde en çok iki ders ve toplamda en fazla
   altı ders çekme hakkı verilir, bu sınır tekrar edilen dersler için
   geçerlidir.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (69.6s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   To initiate the graduation process, students must apply through the
   Student Information System (SIS) within the dates specified in the
   academic calendar. The Student Resources (SR) and the relevant
   Faculties/Institutes must reach full agreement to determine eligible
   graduate candidates. Students monitor their progress each semester via SIS
   to track completed courses, SU and ECTS credits, and remaining
   requirements. If a student does not qualify for graduation in a given
   semester, they must reapply for the next semester. The Graduation Audit
   and Diploma Issuance Procedure outlines the steps for verifying graduation
   requirements, including thesis-related documents for graduate and doctoral
   programs. Institutes submit graduation lists to SR with the approval of
   the Graduate School Director. Diplomas are issued to eligible students who
   complete all necessary procedures, and they can be collected in person or
   sent by registered mail upon request.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (59.3s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The lost and found procedure on campus involves the following steps:  1.
   Individuals who find items should report them by calling extension
   **3565** or delivering them to the **Security Office in Building D2**.  2.
   Security officers prepare a **two-copy Found Item Return Form**, signed by
   both the finder and the security officer. The original remains with the
   Security Unit, and the second copy is given to the finder.  3. Found items
   are categorized as **valuable** (e.g., electronics, money, precious
   metals) or **non-valuable**, placed in labeled bags, and numbered by the
   Security Directorate.  4. Items are retained for **1 year**. For lost
   items, on-campus cases require immediate notification to the **Security
   Unit (via phone)** and the **IT Unit (via their portal)**, while off-
   campus losses require a police report.  5. Security personnel handle
   recording, preservation, and eventual return or disposal of unclaimed
   items.
------------------------------------------------------------

🤖 RAG ANSWER (61.6s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Sabancı University offers several research project fund types:  1.
   **President’s Office Research Fund**: For full-time faculty who started
   within the last two years and completed six months at the university.
   Faculty who previously received an Integration Project can also apply
   once.  2. **Project Research Fund (PAF)**: Composed of accumulated
   research personnel budgets or remaining project funds, used for research
   purposes. It includes overhead amounts transferred to SU for indirect
   contributions.  3. **SOP Fund**: Specifically for undergraduate courses
   (ENS 491/492) in the Industry-Focused Projects Program, involving student-
   led projects with industry partners.  4. **Personal Research Fund (PRF)**:
   A fund for individual researchers, governed by its own procedures to
   maintain SU’s research leadership.   Each fund has distinct eligibility
   criteria and purposes, as outlined in the provided documents.

   doktoru raporu **teslim edildiği gün değerlendirir**. Sorunlu rapor
   sahipleri, **teslimden itibaren 2 gün içinde** SM'ye çağrılır ve ilgili
   hastaneye yönlendirilir.  5. Sorunlu öğrencilerle ilgili yurt yönetimi
   **e-posta ile bilgilendirilir** ve öğrenci mezun olana kadar takip edilir.
   6. Online sistemde akciğer filmi ve kan testi sonuçları kaydedilir. Sorun
   tespit edilen öğrenciler iyileşinceye kadar takip edilir.

🤖 RAG ANSWER (44.5s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   Special event and catering services at Sabancı University are arranged
   through a structured process. Units must submit the **Special Event and
   Catering Request Form** **30 days before the event** via SUForm. The Food
   and Beverage Services (FBS) provides a preliminary offer based on the
   request, including menu specifications, and coordinates with pre-approved
   companies evaluated every 3 years for hygiene and service standards. The
   Procurement and Operations Unit (POP) handles the procurement request (PR)
   after the unit approves the offer. FBS ensures operational details are
   negotiated with the selected company, and all event arrangements are
   supervised by FBS to meet the unit’s requirements. Units cannot request
   advance procurement for future dates beyond the specific event.
------------------------------------------------------------

   Sabancı University's Health Center provides psychiatric and psychological
   examinations for students and employees, with appointments available via
   phone (9923) or email (healthcenter@sabanciuniv.edu). Undergraduate,
   graduate, and doctoral students (excluding Exchange students) are covered
   by health insurance during their education period, which activates at the
   start of the school year and remains valid until graduation. Students with
   referrals approved by the Health Center Physician can access private
   health services at contracted institutions, though inpatient treatments
   (e.g., surgery) do not require a referral. Preventive health services
   include mandatory tests during enrollment, such as complete blood count,
   Hepatitis A, Hepatitis B, and MMR screenings. Emergency health services
   are supported by a fully equipped ambulance team, operating in shifts with
   a doctor, anesthesiologist, and driver. During pandemics, the Health
   Center coordinates transport to designated hospitals and manages
   quarantine for close contacts via announcements.

   çizelgesi), bütçesi (50.000 TL sabit destek) ve harcama kalemleri (makine
   alımı, seyahat giderleri 300 USD/gün, doktora öğrencisi çalışma giderleri
   hariç) detaylı olarak yer almalıdır. Ayrıca, alt yapı kurulumu için ek
   50.000 TL makine ve teçhizat alımı bütçesi talep edilebilir. Desteği alan
   PY’lar, projenin sonuna kadar en az iki dış kaynaklı hibe başvurusu veya

🤖 RAG ANSWER (68.3s):
------------------------------------------------------------
   The Rectorate Research Fund (SU President’s Office Research Fund) requires
   applicants to meet the following conditions:  - Full-time faculty members
   must have started their activities at Sabancı University within the last
   two years and completed at least six months at the institution.  - By the
   end of the project, they must have submitted at least two new documented
   external grant applications (e.g., to ERC, Horizon Europe, TÜBİTAK) or
   secured one guaranteed external financing.  - They must publish high-
   impact scientific articles during the project.  - The project duration
   cannot exceed 36 months, with a maximum budget of 300,000 TL for
   theoretical research and 600,000 TL for experimental research.  - A
   project is classified as experimental if laboratory equipment,
   consumables, or experimental field study costs exceed 30% of the total
   budget.  - Faculty members are eligible to apply only once, regardless of
   prior receipt of an Integration Project.  - Applications must align with
   the applicant’s existing or ongoing research activities.  - The deadline
   for applications is May 31 of each year.
------------------------------------------------------------
